  The Center is directed and guided by its membership of all classes and is the only known
organization with actual full participation of the membership.


  Requests for information or contributions by individuals not members of the Center or from
other organizations are welcomed.

 ing upri ght . A l egend grows wi th each tel l i ng of th e tal e ,   bu t so far the mons ter has
